This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am the independent Research Reviewer (独立科学审查员) for the `symb_invariant_consensus` project targeting EMNLP 2026 ARR (May 25 deadline). My ongoing responsibilities are:
   - Independently verify experimental conclusions using read-only tools (registry, ssh_read_only, wandb, logs)
   - Review ROADMAP updates for research direction soundness, claim validity, and methodological correctness
   - Post findings via `reviewer_post_to_director` with severity levels (info/warning/critical)
   - Monitor running experiments for data quality, resource compliance, and GPU allocation
   - Catch data inconsistencies, worker misreports, and methodological issues before they enter the paper
   - All output must be in Chinese

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Symbolic Confirmation Bias (SCB)**: Self-extracted constraints project model bias. BR>1 means biased. Core thesis of the paper.
   - **BR (Bias Ratio)**: weight_wrong / weight_gold for SICA-wrong questions. Self-extraction BR: Mistral 4.97, Qwen 3.93, LLaMA 2.88, gpt-4o 2.94. Cross-model BR: R1→Mistral 2.63, gpt-4.1→Mistral 2.70.
   - **SICA vs SC**: SICA uses Z3 MAX-SAT constraint aggregation; SC is majority voting over K=12 traces. Same-model: SICA ≤ SC (bias). Cross-model: depends on Process-κ and capability gap.
   - **Process-κ**: TF-IDF cosine similarity of reasoning traces. Key predictor of cross-model escape success. R1-Distill: 0.252 (diverse, escape works), gpt-4.1: 0.792 (homogeneous, escape fails), gpt-4o: 0.788 (homogeneous).
   - **Cross-Model Extraction**: Using a different model to extract FOL constraints. R1→Mistral: +2.15pp (success). gpt-4.1→Mistral: -1.47pp (failure). Two necessary conditions: (a) extractor capability match (gap < ~20pp threshold), (b) source trace diversity (low Process-κ).
   - **BR=∞ rate**: Fraction of SICA-wrong problems where gold answer has zero constraint weight. Increases with model capability: gpt-4.1 58%, gpt-4o 44%, Mistral 30%.
   - **D135 Narrative**: Cross-model extraction is "necessary but not sufficient" — requires both capability match AND trace diversity.
   - **BPE Bug**: vLLM 0.21.0 + R1-Distill outputs raw BPE characters (Ġ=space, Ċ=newline), causing JSON parsing failures. Root cause of 0% self-extraction rate and broken SC=32.35% (actual SC=73.12% with BPE decode).
   - **Servers**: westd-16639 (4× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 96GB, main GPU experiments), westd-25068 (4× same GPUs, currently downloading corrupt model shards for 70B deployment).

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/exp_r1_distill_8b_sica/metrics.json`** (westd-16639)
     - CRITICAL: Contains wrong metadata `exp_id: "exp-d126-70b-sica"` and `model: "Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ"` but actual data is R1-Distill self-extraction (SC=32.35%, extraction_rate=1.28%)
     - Pipeline rerun inherited wrong config from vLLM port 8002 auto-detection
   
   - **`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/exp_d135_frontier_scb/gpt-4.1/cross_model/gpt41_full_metrics.json`** (westd-16639)
     - Complete gpt-4.1 cross-model metrics: `{"model": "gpt-4.1", "dataset": "FOLIO-204", "extraction_model": "Mistral-7B", "sc_accuracy": 0.8284, "sica_accuracy": 0.8137, "delta_pp": -1.47, "extraction_rate": 1.0, "n_sica_wrong": 38, "mean_br": 2.697, "median_br": 1.534, "weighted_br": 3.2099, "n_br_inf": 22, "process_kappa": 0.7916, "answer_fleiss_kappa": 0.862}`
   
   - **`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/exp_d135_frontier_scb/gpt-4.1/cross_model/cross_model_mistral.json`** (westd-16639)
     - 204 problem entries with cross_model_correct, sc_correct, constraint stats per problem
     - 166/204 cross-model correct, 169/204 SC correct

     - R1-Distill cross-model summary: n_problems=93, sc_accuracy=0.7312, sica_accuracy=0.7527, delta_pp=2.15, extraction_rate=0.9319, mean_br=2.6293, process_kappa_tfidf=0.2522
   
   - **`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/exp_r1_distill_8b_sica/pipeline_rerun.log`** (westd-16639)

   - **ROADMAP.md** — Updated 3 times during this session:
     - D135 capability-gap narrative shift
     - Process-κ added as predictive variable per Reviewer suggestion
     - gpt-4.1 cross BR removed from self-extraction section per Reviewer warning
     - Final version: Self-extraction BR (4 models: 2.88-4.97×), Cross-model table with Gap + Process-κ + Δ columns

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **ssh_read_only command blocking**: Commands with `>`, `|`, or `2>/dev/null` were blocked by read-only mode. Fixed by restructuring commands to avoid shell metacharacters (e.g., using `ps -p PID -o pid,etime,comm` instead of piped commands).
   
   - **R1-Distill metrics.json metadata contamination**: `exp_id` and `model` fields wrong. Reported as [severity: warning] to Director. Not yet fixed in file but noted for analysis scripts.
   
   - **Worker misreport — wrong server (AGAIN)**: `worker_experiment_r1-distill-remaining-extraction` claimed data was lost on westd-25068, but R1-Distill data was always on westd-16639. Worker said "项目绑定服务器是 westd-25068" but went to wrong server. This is a repeat of the same pattern from prior context. I independently verified all data intact on westd-16639.
   
   - **70B westd-25068 deployment failed**: 18/37 safetensors shards corrupted (original download when disk was 100% full). Worker started re-downloading from ModelScope but timed out. vLLM crashed on startup due to incomplete model. Download still in progress (19 finalized, 8 in batch 1 downloading at ~1.7-3MB/s).
   
   - **Cron auto-fail false positives**: Both exp-d135-frontier-scb and exp-d126-r1-distill-sica triggered cron on_failed because tracked PIDs exited, but experiments were actually progressing normally. Registry notes updated to explain.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Discovered gpt-4.1 cross-model SICA < SC (-1.47pp)**: Major finding that cross-model extraction doesn't always help. Analyzed root cause as dual: (a) capability gap too large (27pp), (b) trace homogeneity too high (Process-κ=0.79). This led to D135 narrative revision.
   
   - **Identified Process-κ as the key predictive variable**: R1-Distill uniquely low Process-κ (0.252) explains why it's the only model where cross-model SICA > SC. This was adopted into ROADMAP narrative.
   
   - **Caught ROADMAP methodological mixing**: gpt-4.1 cross-model BR (2.70) was listed under "Self-extraction SCB" — flagged and corrected.
   
   - **Confirmed R1-Distill pipeline rerun completion + cross-extraction progress**: Pipeline finished 204 problems (FULL_MVP_DONE), and Mistral cross-extraction for remaining ~111 problems is actively running (>128/204 files in per_trace_constraints_v2).

        "content": "{\n  \"raw\": \"完整指标表：\\n\\n| 指标 | gpt-4.1 | gpt-4o (参照) |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| SC accuracy | 82.84% (169/204) | 73.91% |\\n| SICA accuracy | 81.37% (166/204) | 72.05% |\\n| Δ (SICA-SC) | -1.47pp | -1.86pp |\\n| Mean BR | **2.697** | 2.94 |\\n| Median BR | **1.534** | 1.91 |\\n| Weighted BR | **3.2099** | 3.07 |\\n| n_br_finite | **16** (38-22) | 25 |\\n| BR=∞ | **22** | 20 |\\n| extraction_rate | **1.0000** | — |\\n| Process-κ | 0.792 | 0.788 |\\n| Answer-κ | 0.862 | 0.801 |\\n\\n要点：\\n- Mean/Median BR 比 gpt-4o 低（2.70 vs 2.94, 1.53 vs 1.91），说明 gpt-4.1 在 SICA-wrong 题上的偏差程度更温和\\n- Weighted BR 略高（3.21 vs 3.07），因为高权重题目上偏差更大\\n- BR=∞ 占比更高（22/38=57.9% vs 20/45=44.4%），gold answer 零权重的情况更多——n_br_finite 只有 16 题\\n- extraction_rate = 100%，所有 trace 都成功提取了约束\\n\\n结果已保存到 `/root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/exp_d135_frontier_scb/gpt-4.1/cross_model/gpt41_full_metrics.json`。\"\n}",

SC baseline 最高，capability trap 确认。跨 seed SC vs delta Pearson r=-0.8973。\"}, \"exp-050b-mistral-icl-oracle\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T23:19:54.810Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"ICL Oracle (5 gold FOL examples) on Mistral-7B FOLIO-199: SICA=54.27% vs SC=57.79%, delta=-3.52pp (p=0.167, ns). ICL boosted SC from vanilla 54.41% (exp-033) to 57.79% (+3.38pp), but SICA did not benefit. ICL improves individual trace quality enough to boost majority vote, but the resulting constraints become less diverse/discriminative for MaxSAT weighting. Confirms capability trap: better extraction quality does not translate to SICA gains when it also reduces beneficial disagreement.\"}, \"exp-050-mistral-icl-oracle-folio199\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T23:19:53.802Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp-049b-mistral-logiqa200\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T23:16:15.080Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Mistral-7B LogiQA-200: SICA=51.50% vs SC=51.00%, delta=+0.50pp (p=1.000, ns). Mistral's +4.90pp FOLIO advantage (exp-033) does not transfer to LogiQA. SC baseline 51% is near random for 4-choice MCQ. Consistent with exp-038 (14B LogiQA delta=-0.50pp): SICA provides no benefit on LogiQA regardless of model, likely because LogiQA's pragmatic reasoning resists symbolic constraint formalization.\"}, \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:30:00.573Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"v1: 可能使用了 Qwen-7B 残留 vLLM (exp-035 遗留 port 8003)。BV=53.92% vs SC=75.00%, delta=-21.08pp。Z3 SAT rate 95.3%=无判别力。True bias 72.5% vs gold 35.3%。根因：backward FOL generation 质量同样差，验证方向不解决 capability trap。即使换正确模型 (-21pp 幅度过大) 可能仍 negative。建议 Director 决策是否用 Qwen2.5-14B 重跑 v2。\"}, \"exp-038-14b-logiqa200-16639\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:27:39.296Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"实验成功完成200/200题，之前被自动监控误判为failed。结果：SICA略逊于SC(-0.5%)，McNemar p=1.0完全不显著。仅3例disagreement(sica_only=1, sc_only=2)。extraction_rate=86.4%低于FOLIO实验(~90%+)，fail_empty=12.2%较高——LogiQA的非形式化推理对constraint extraction更难。contradiction_rate仅0.8%，几乎无矛盾检测。结论：SICA在LogiQA非形式逻辑推理上无增益，capability trap假设在此数据集上未验证。\"}, \"exp-033\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:25:06.703Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Mistral-7B FOLIO-204: SICA 59.31% vs SC 54.41%, delta=+4.90pp (McNemar p=0.013, significant). 首次 SICA 显著超越 SC。关键因素：C0=81.37%（远高于 Qwen-7B 的 55%），高 diversity 使 SC majority vote 退化，而 SICA MaxSAT 仍能从 noisy traces 中提取信号。Discordant: SICA-only=12, SC-only=2, 高度不对称。但绝对准确率低（59% vs Qwen-7B 77%），说明 Mistral-7B 基础推理能力弱。结论：capability trap 非普遍现象，依赖 diversity/quality 平衡——高 diversity + 低 quality 时 SICA 有优势。\"}, \"exp-042b-premise-formal\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:10:14.058Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"失败根因：run_premise_formalization.sh 参数顺序是 PORT GPU，但调用传了 GPU PORT (2 8002)，导致 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=8002 → NVMLError_InvalidArgument。vLLM 未启动，0 题完成。将在 cleanup Worker 中用显式命令重部署。\"}, \"exp-044-icl-oracle\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:54:58.560Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"v2 失败: vLLM server 连接中断 (httpx.ConnectError: All connection attempts failed)，EXIT_CODE=129 (SIGHUP)。实验跑完但数据无效：extraction 成功率仅 17.1% (307/1800, 1490 json_fail)，SC=46.23% 远低于 Qwen2.5-14B 预期 75.49%。结果不可用，需在 vLLM 稳定后重跑。worker_infra_westd25068-gpu-cleanup-redeploy 正在处理 westd-25068 基础设施。\"}, \"exp-035\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:07:57.772Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"v2 完成: Qwen2.5-7B FOLIO-204, 203/204题有结果(1题缺失)。JSON summary是stale的(n=195时计算)，实际数据从results数组203条计算。SICA与SC仅3个discordant pairs，几乎完全一致。假设验证：SICA无法outperform SC on 7B，delta=-0.49pp (p=1.0)。C0=0.55高于14B但未能转化为SICA优势。注意7B SC(77.34%)意外高于14B SC(75.49%)，可能因FOLIO数据特性。\"}, \"exp-036b-cross-trace-unsat\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:04:55.982Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Cross-trace UNSAT 验证了 Director 假设：cross-answer UNSAT rate (8.7%) 约为 within-trace (0.9%) 的 10x。但主要瓶颈是 Z3 变量命名不一致：56.4% 的 answer pairs 共享零变量，Z3 无法跨 trace 比较。False-True 对最具辨别力 (non-empty 中 46.7% UNSAT)。最终对准确率无影响（仅覆盖 9/204 问题），第 6 个 negative constructive。核心诊断价值：变量命名不一致是 capability trap 的关键机制之一。\"}, \"exp-042-premise-formalization\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:02:37.081Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp-043-consistency-filtering\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:59:10.069Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Consistency filtering NEGATIVE: 过滤后约束数量骤降（T≥4 仅 1.8/题, T≥8 仅 0.3/题），MaxSAT 无法区分答案，退化为 SC。根因：Z3 变量命名不一致导致语义等价约束被视为不同（avg 13.2 unique constraints/problem），跨 trace 一致性极低。第 5 个 negative constructive intervention。注意：T=1 的 77.94% 与 exp-001 的 74.51% 差异是已知 pipeline 非确定性问题（code path confound）。\"}, \"exp-036-z3-feedback-folio204\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:53:05.656Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z3 feedback mechanism had zero real impact. Apparent +3.0pp (77.5% vs 74.5%) entirely due to code path confound: JSON serialization vs in-memory processing caused different dedup/scoring behavior. UNSAT trigger rate 0.9% (22/2448 traces), too low for feedback loop to meaningfully activate. All observed delta on discordant problems came from pipeline non-determinism, not Z3 refinement. Fourth negative constructive intervention after contrastive, cross-model, generational.\"}, \"exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:06:48.306Z\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"notes\": \"Killed per D049: extraction_rate 73% below 80% threshold. Partial results (35/204): SICA 88.57%, SC 85.71%, delta +2.86pp. But extraction_rate degrading and ETA 16h+ made continued run not worthwhile. GPU freed for D050 constructive experiments.\"}, \"exp-033-qwen3-pw600-nonthinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:00:09.156Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: AutoDL 平台级 SIGTERM 导致 vLLM EngineCore 崩溃 (00:05:54)，后紧急清理 Worker 误杀 tmux server 连带终止。518/600 intermediates 有效可恢复。\"}, \"exp-004-qwen7b-rerun\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:00:08.383Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: ModelScope 下载失败，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 模型下载未完成。实验从未开始运行。需要 HF 镜像修复后重试。\"}, \"exp-007-qwen72b-awq\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:00:07.574Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: HuggingFace 网络不通 (Network unreachable)，模型下载失败。72B AWQ 模型从未下载完成，实验从未开始运行。需要 HF 镜像修复后重试。\"}, \"exp-027-qwen3-14b-non-thinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T17:58:11.787Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Qwen3-14B non-thinking FOLIO-204: SICA 84.31% vs SC 83.82%, delta=+0.49pp。Qwen3 SC baseline 83.82% 远高于 Qwen2.5-14B 的 75.00% (+8.82pp)，证明 Qwen3 基础能力显著提升。但 SICA-SC gap 仍≈0 (+0.49pp)，capability trap 在次世代模型上持续。Extraction rate 99.5% (vs 14B 98.5%)，contradiction rate 2.83%。论文价值：(1) 证明 capability trap 是结构性的，非模型能力不足；(2) 代际升级提升 SC 不提升 SICA delta。\"}, \"exp-030-crossmodel-mistral-folio204\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T16:16:11.345Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"NEGATIVE. Cross-model extraction (Mistral-7B extracts constraints from Qwen2.5-14B traces) 完全无效：SICA = SC = 75.00%, discriminative_rate=0%, recovery_rate=0%. Mistral 虽然是不同模型家族，但提取的 constraints 同样缺乏判别力。这进一步强化 capability trap 结论：问题不在 same-model bias，而在 LLM extraction 本身的能力限制——无论哪个模型做 extraction，都无法从 CoT traces 中提取出对答案有判别力的 symbolic constraints。论文 claim: cross-model 不是 rescue path。\"}, \"exp-031-oracle-folio-regen\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T15:55:28.070Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"KEY RESULT. Oracle-SICA (gold FOL + Z3 entailment + consistency-weighted voting) 89.71% vs SC 75.49% vs SICA(self) 74.51%, +14.22pp over SC, McNemar p=2.0e-07 极显著, b=29 c=0 (oracle 从未输给 SC)。Z3 gold agreement 95.10% (194/204), FOL parser 204/204 全成功。Per-type: True 97.2% (+4.1pp), False 93.6% (+6.3pp), Unknown 78.3% (+31.9pp)。与 exp-021 (88.24%, 旧 traces) 对比：oracle 准确率从 88.24% 提升至 89.71%（+1.47pp），delta 从 +8.82pp 增至 +14.22pp。差异来源：(1) exp-026 traces 的 SC baseline 更低 (75.49% vs 79.41%)；(2) Z3 agreement 从 94.6% 提升至 95.1%。结论与 exp-021 一致：MAX-SAT 聚合机制有效——瓶颈在约束提取质量。\"}, \"exp-029-contrastive-folio204\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T15:38:58.925Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\"}, \"exp-026-folio204-trace-regen\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T14:46:34.862Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"FOLIO 204 全量 trace 重生成完成 (204/204)。SICA 74.51% vs SC 75.00%, delta=-0.49pp，与 sica_expanded_14b FOLIO 子集 (79.4%/79.4%, delta=0) 一致——capability trap 在重生成 traces 上仍成立。归一化 bug 修复 + 温度双参数分离后 extraction_rate=98.53% (vs 之前 89%)。Traces 已被 exp-029 contrastive 实验复用。结果路径: /root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/folio_204_14b/folio_204_results.json\"}, \"exp-023-discriminative-rate\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T04:44:24.397Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Full 804-instance discriminative rate (R3 W1+W3, v1+v2 合并). By weight: 39.2% [38.7, 39.8]; by count (est): 12.1% [12.0, 12.3]. Z3 semantic disc: 8.2% [7.6, 8.8]. Per-domain: FOLIO 54.0% > PW 36.4% (by weight). 289/307 multi-candidate 问题有≥1 discriminative constraint (94.1%). Pilot validation: weight 37.6% vs 报告 37.2% (delta 0.38pp); count 12.1% vs 报告 11.5% (delta 0.6pp)——高度一致。v2 修复了 v1 的 assertion weight 不对称问题。数据: /root/symb_invariant_consensus/analysis/discriminative_rate_full_804.json\"}, \"exp-022-oracle-proofwriter\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T03:48:13.174Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"KEY RESULT. ICL boosted SC from vanilla 54.41% (exp-033) to 57.79% (+3.38pp), but SICA did not benefit. ICL improves individual trace quality enough to boost majority vote, but the resulting constraints become less diverse/discriminative for MaxSAT weighting. Confirms capability trap: better extraction quality does not translate to SICA gains when it also reduces beneficial disagreement.\\\"}, \\\"exp-050-mistral-icl-oracle-folio199\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T23:19:53.802Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp-049b-mistral-logiqa200\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T23:16:15.080Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mistral-7B LogiQA-200: SICA=51.50% vs SC=51.00%, delta=+0.50pp (p=1.000, ns). Mistral's +4.90pp FOLIO advantage (exp-033) does not transfer to LogiQA. SC baseline 51% is near random for 4-choice MCQ. Consistent with exp-038 (14B LogiQA delta=-0.50pp): SICA provides no benefit on LogiQA regardless of model, likely because LogiQA's pragmatic reasoning resists symbolic constraint formalization.\\\"}, {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T21:53:04.043Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp-041-14b-folio204-16639\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T21:08:19.597Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"exp-045b-backward-verify\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T20:30:28.631Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\"}, \\\"exp-045-backward-verification\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T20:30:00.573Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"v1: 可能使用了 Qwen-7B 残留 vLLM (exp-035 遗留 port 8003)。BV=53.92% vs SC=75.00%, delta=-21.08pp。Z3 SAT rate 95.3%=无判别力。True bias 72.5% vs gold 35.3%。根因：backward FOL generation 质量同样差，验证方向不解决 capability trap。即使换正确模型 (-21pp 幅度过大) 可能仍 negative。建议 Director 决策是否用 Qwen2.5-14B 重跑 v2。\\\"}, \\\"exp-038-14b-logiqa200-16639\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T20:27:39.296Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"实验成功完成200/200题，之前被自动监控误判为failed。结果：SICA略逊于SC(-0.5%)，McNemar p=1.0完全不显著。仅3例disagreement(sica_only=1, sc_only=2)。extraction_rate=86.4%低于FOLIO实验(~90%+)，fail_empty=12.2%较高——LogiQA的非形式化推理对constraint extraction更难。contradiction_rate仅0.8%，几乎无矛盾检测。结论：SICA在LogiQA非形式逻辑推理上无增益，capability trap假设在此数据集上未验证。\\\"}, \\\"exp-033\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T20:25:06.703Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mistral-7B FOLIO-204: SICA 59.31% vs SC 54.41%, delta=+4.90pp (McNemar p=0.013, significant). 首次 SICA 显著超越 SC。关键因素：C0=81.37%（远高于 Qwen-7B 的 55%），高 diversity 使 SC majority vote 退化，而 SICA MaxSAT 仍能从 noisy traces 中提取信号。Discordant: SICA-only=12, SC-only=2, 高度不对称。但绝对准确率低（59% vs Qwen-7B 77%），说明 Mistral-7B 基础推理能力弱。结论：capability trap 非普遍现象，依赖 diversity/quality 平衡——高 diversity + 低 quality 时 SICA 有优势。\\\"}, \\\"exp-042b-premise-formal\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T20:10:14.058Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"失败根因：run_premise_formalization.sh 参数顺序是 PORT GPU，但调用传了 GPU PORT (2 8002)，导致 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=8002 → NVMLError_InvalidArgument。vLLM 未启动，0 题完成。将在 cleanup Worker 中用显式命令重部署。\\\"}, \\\"exp-044-icl-oracle\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T19:54:58.560Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"v2 失败: vLLM server 连接中断 (httpx.ConnectError: All connection attempts failed)，EXIT_CODE=129 (SIGHUP)。实验跑完但数据无效：extraction 成功率仅 17.1% (307/1800, 1490 json_fail)，SC=46.23% 远低于 Qwen2.5-14B 预期 75.49%。结果不可用，需在 vLLM 稳定后重跑。worker_infra_westd25068-gpu-cleanup-redeploy 正在处理 westd-25068 基础设施。\\\"}, \\\"exp-035\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T19:07:57.772Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"v2 完成: Qwen2.5-7B FOLIO-204, 203/204题有结果(1题缺失)。JSON summary是stale的(n=195时计算)，实际数据从results数组203条计算。SICA与SC仅3个discordant pairs，几乎完全一致。假设验证：SICA无法outperform SC on 7B，delta=-0.49pp (p=1.0)。C0=0.55高于14B但未能转化为SICA优势。注意7B SC(77.34%)意外高于14B SC(75.49%)，可能因FOLIO数据特性。\\\"}, \\\"exp-036b-cross-trace-unsat\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T19:04:55.982Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Cross-trace UNSAT 验证了 Director 假设：cross-answer UNSAT rate (8.7%) 约为 within-trace (0.9%) 的 10x。但主要瓶颈是 Z3 变量命名不一致：56.4% 的 answer pairs 共享零变量，Z3 无法跨 trace 比较。False-True 对最具辨别力 (non-empty 中 46.7% UNSAT)。最终对准确率无影响（仅覆盖 9/204 问题），第 6 个 negative constructive。核心诊断价值：变量命名不一致是 capability trap 的关键机制之一。\\\"}, \\\"exp-042-premise-formalization\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T19:02:37.081Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp-043-consistency-filtering\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T18:59:10.069Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Consistency filtering NEGATIVE: 过滤后约束数量骤降（T≥4 仅 1.8/题, T≥8 仅 0.3/题），MaxSAT 无法区分答案，退化为 SC。根因：Z3 变量命名不一致导致语义等价约束被视为不同（avg 13.2 unique constraints/problem），跨 trace 一致性极低。第 5 个 negative constructive intervention。注意：T=1 的 77.94% 与 exp-001 的 74.51% 差异是已知 pipeline 非确定性问题（code path confound）。\\\"}, \\\"exp-036-z3-feedback-folio204\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T18:53:05.656Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Z3 feedback mechanism had zero real impact. Apparent +3.0pp (77.5% vs 74.5%) entirely due to code path confound: JSON serialization vs in-memory processing caused different dedup/scoring behavior. UNSAT trigger rate 0.9% (22/2448 traces), too low for feedback loop to meaningfully activate. All observed delta on discordant problems came from pipeline non-determinism, not Z3 refinement. Fourth negative constructive intervention after contrastive, cross-model, generational.\\\"}, \\\"exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T18:06:48.306Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Killed per D049: extraction_rate 73% below 80% threshold. Partial results (35/204): SICA 88.57%, SC 85.71%, delta +2.86pp. But extraction_rate degrading and ETA 16h+ made continued run not worthwhile. GPU freed for D050 constructive experiments.\\\"}, \\\"exp-033-qwen3-pw600-nonthinking\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T18:00:09.156Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"FAILED: AutoDL 平台级 SIGTERM 导致 vLLM EngineCore 崩溃 (00:05:54)，后紧急清理 Worker 误杀 tmux server 连带终止。518/600 intermediates 有效可恢复。\\\"}, \\\"exp-004-qwen7b-rerun\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T18:00:08.383Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"FAILED: ModelScope 下载失败，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 模型下载未完成。实验从未开始运行。需要 HF 镜像修复后重试。\\\"}, \\\"exp-007-qwen72b-awq\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T18:00:07.574Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"FAILED: HuggingFace 网络不通 (Network unreachable)，模型下载失败。72B AWQ 模型从未下载完成，实验从未开始运行。需要 HF 镜像修复后重试。\\\"}, \\\"exp-027-qwen3-14b-non-thinking\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T17:58:11.787Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Qwen3-14B non-thinking FOLIO-204: SICA 84.31% vs SC 83.82%, delta=+0.49pp。Qwen3 SC baseline 83.82% 远高于 Qwen2.5-14B 的 75.00% (+8.82pp)，证明 Qwen3 基础能力显著提升。但 SICA-SC gap 仍≈0 (+0.49pp)，capability trap 在次世代模型上持续。Extraction rate 99.5% (vs 14B 98.5%)，contradiction rate 2.83%。论文价值：(1) 证明 capability trap 是结构性的，非模型能力不足；(2) 代际升级提升 SC 不提升 SICA delta。\\\"}, \\\"exp-030-crossmodel-mistral-folio204\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T16:16:11.345Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"NEGATIVE. Cross-model extraction (Mistral-7B extracts constraints from Qwen2.5-14B traces) 完全无效：SICA = SC = 75.00%, discriminative_rate=0%, recovery_rate=0%. Mistral 虽然是不同模型家族，但提取的 constraints 同样缺乏判别力。这进一步强化 capability trap 结论：问题不在 same-model bias，而在 LLM extraction 本身的能力限制——无论哪个模型做 extraction，都无法从 CoT traces 中提取出对答案有判别力的 symbolic constraints。论文 claim: cross-model 不是 rescue path。\\\"}, \\\"exp-031-oracle-folio-regen\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T15:55:28.070Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"KEY RESULT. Oracle-SICA (gold FOL + Z3 entailment + consistency-weighted voting) 89.71% vs SC 75.49% vs SICA(self) 74.51%, +14.22pp over SC, McNemar p=2.0e-07 极显著, b=29 c=0 (oracle 从未输给 SC)。Z3 gold agreement 95.10% (194/204), FOL parser 204/204 全成功。Per-type: True 97.2% (+4.1pp), False 93.6% (+6.3pp), Unknown 78.3% (+31.9pp)。与 exp-021 (88.24%, 旧 traces) 对比：oracle 准确率从 88.24% 提升至 89.71%（+1.47pp），delta 从 +8.82pp 增至 +14.22pp。差异来源：(1) exp-026 traces 的 SC baseline 更低 (75.49% vs 79.41%)；(2) Z3 agreement 从 94.6% 提升至 95.1%。结论与 exp-021 一致：MAX-SAT 聚合机制有效——瓶颈在约束提取质量。\\\"}, \\\"exp-029-contrastive-folio204\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T15:38:58.925Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"exp-026-folio204-trace-regen\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T14:46:34.862Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"FOLIO 204 全量 trace 重生成完成 (204/204)。SICA 74.51% vs SC 75.00%, delta=-0.49pp，与 sica_expanded_14b FOLIO 子集 (79.4%/79.4%, delta=0) 一致——capability trap 在重生成 traces 上仍成立。归一化 bug 修复 + 温度双参数分离后 extraction_rate=98.53% (vs 之前 89%)。Traces 已被 exp-029 contrastive 实验复用。结果路径: /root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/folio_204_14b/folio_204_results.json\\\"}, \\\"exp-023-discriminative-rate\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T04:44:24.397Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Full 804-instance discriminative rate (R3 W1+W3, v1+v2 合并). By weight: 39.2% [38.7, 39.8]; by count (est): 12.1% [12.0, 12.3]. Z3 semantic disc: 8.2% [7.6, 8.8]. Per-domain: FOLIO 54.0% > PW 36.4% (by weight). 289/307 multi-candidate 问题有≥1 discriminative constraint (94.1%). Pilot validation: weight 37.6% vs 报告 37.2% (delta 0.38pp); count 12.1% vs 报告 11.5% (delta 0.6pp)——高度一致。v2 修复了 v1 的 assertion weight 不对称问题。数据: /root/symb_invariant_consensus/analysis/discriminative_rate_full_804.json\\\"}, \\\"exp-022-oracle-proofwriter\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-18T03:48:13.174Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"KEY RESULT. ProofWriter Oracle-SICA (gold DSL + Z3 + MAX-SAT) 86.67% vs SC 75.17% vs SICA(self) 75.50%, +11.50pp, McNemar p=9.21e-15 极显著。Z3 gold agreement 97.14% (577/594), parse errors 仅 6/600 (1%), solver errors 0/7128。Per-subtype: RelNeg +17.86pp > RelNoneg +13.01pp > AttNoneg +9.09pp > AttNeg +6.87pp——关系谓词（推理链更长）获益远大于属性谓词。与 FOLIO oracle (exp-021, +8.82pp, p=6.15e-5) 对比：ProofWriter delta 更大 (+11.50 vs +8.82) 且统计显著性高 6 个数量级。两个子域 Oracle 实验一致证明：MAX-SAT 聚合机制有效，瓶颈在 LLM self-extraction 质量。\\\"}, \\\"temperature-ablation-t07\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T19:11:20.157Z\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"T=0.7 同源补跑完成（60/60 题）。C0=40.0%，介于 T=0.5(38.3%) 和 T=1.0(43.3%) 之间，消除了旧数据 T=0.7(53.3%) 来自不同 run 的混淆。四温度点 C0 现呈单调递增：T=0.3(30%) → T=0.5(38.3%) → T=0.7(40.0%) → T=1.0(43.3%)。SICA 71.67% < SC 75.00%，delta=-3.33pp，discordant 0:2（SC wins only）。FOLIO 80%=80%，PW SICA 63.33% < SC 70%。结论与其他温度点一致：diversity 增加但 constraint quality 无改善，delta≈0。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"sica_constraint_ablation_013\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T18:38:00.543Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"exp-013 constraint ablation 完成，数据已从 ablation_results.json 交叉验证。关键修正：(1) 802/804 不存在于任何 pairwise comparison，原 registry 数据错误；(2) random-weight accuracy 修正为 75.87% (610/804)，原 76.37% 是近似值。核心发现：MaxSAT vs Count-only 803/804 agreement 确认 SAT solver 无增量。MaxSAT vs SC 773/804 (31 disagreements, 16 SICA wins / 14 SC wins / 1 both wrong diff)——SICA 改了 31 题但 wins/losses 几乎抵消。数据文件：/root/symb_invariant_consensus/analysis/exp013_agreement_matrix.json\\\"}, \\\"exp-004-qwen7b\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T18:10:04.422Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Qwen2.5-7B within-family scaling: SICA=SC=68.33% (41/60), Δ=0.00pp, C0=43.3%, McNemar p=1.0。60 题全部完成（run.log 确认）。C0=43.3% 高于 14B 的 38.2%（弱模型更多 diversity），但 Δ=0 确认 capability trap 在 7B scale 同样成立。Discordant 2:2 完全对称。Per-domain: FOLIO SICA 76.7% vs SC 73.3% (+3.3pp), PW SICA 60.0% vs SC 63.3% (-3.3pp)，方向相反互相抵消。extraction_rate=94.2%。\\\"}, \\\"temperature-ablation-t10\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T17:16:54.736Z\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"T=1.0 高温：C0=43.3%，低于 T=0.7 的 53.3%（注意：T=0.7 数据来自 sica_d012_14b 不同 run）。SICA=SC=75.00%，delta=0，discordant=0。最大温度仍不能拯救 SICA。Temperature ablation 结论：T=0.3(30%) → T=0.5(38.3%) → T=1.0(43.3%) 三点 C0 递增，但加上 T=0.7(53.3%, 来自 sica_d012_14b) 后整体非单调（T=0.7 > T=1.0）。不同 run 间 C0 有随机波动。核心结论不变：delta 始终=0，diversity 不是瓶颈。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\"}, \\\"exp-012-gsm8k-pilot\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T17:09:35.283Z\\\", constraints 难以提取。支持 C0 作为 SICA 无效性预测指标。\\\"}, \\\"exp-021-oracle-folio\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T16:48:21.295Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"KEY RESULT. Oracle-SICA (gold FOL + Z3 consistency check + MAX-SAT) 88.24% vs SC 79.41% vs SICA(self-extracted) 79.41%, +8.82pp, McNemar p=6.15e-05 高度显著。Z3 solver 与 gold answer 一致率 94.6%（193/204），FOL parser 204/204 全部成功。SICA(self)=SC 确认 LLM 自提取约束在 formal logic 上无有效信号。结论：MAX-SAT 聚合机制本身有效——瓶颈在约束提取质量，不在聚合算法。这直接回答了 Reviewer 的核心 confound 质疑。\\\"}, \\\"temperature-ablation-t05\\\": {\\\"status\\\": instruction-scale model。SICA -3.3pp，两子域对称（FOLIO -3.3pp, PW -3.3pp）。重新定位为第四个 instruction-scale 模型验证 capability trap。\\\"}, \\\"exp-010-mistral-v3\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T13:39:59.922Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mistral-7B cross-family: SICA 48.33% = SC 48.33%, delta=0.00pp。Multi-candidate rate 50%（30/60 题非 unanimous），SICA 仅改变 4 题（6.67% flip rate）。模型太弱：SC baseline 48.33%（近随机），trace 缺乏质量但有多样性。Discordant 2:2 平局，McNemar p=1.0。支持 operating regime 有下界：模型必须足够强才能生成有意义的 diverse traces。PW 40%/40%, FOLIO 56.67%/56.67%。\\\"}, \\\"sica_expanded_14b\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T13:26:43.835Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"实验完整完成 \\\"exp-019-llama8b-strategyqa-200\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T13:26:41.871Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"StrategyQA 200题 powered test: SICA 74% vs SC 72%, +2.00pp, McNemar p=0.424, bootstrap CI [-1.5, +5.5] 跨零。统计不显著。exp-011 的 +6.67pp 在扩大样本后缩水 70%——小样本噪声。关键发现：(1) LLaMA-8B 在 StrategyQA 上 SC=72%，高于预期的 sweet spot (55-65%)，(2) 仅 14/200 (7%) discordant pairs，54.5% 全票一致，(3) SICA 仅充当 tiebreaker（9 wins 中 6 个是平票）。StrategyQA 不在 operating regime sweet spot 内。exp-020 FOLIO (SC≈60%) 是真正的 definitive test。\\\"}, \\\"exp-018-arc-v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T13:26:39.502Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"ARC-Challenge cross-domain: SICA 95.00% vs SC 96.67%, gap=-1.67pp. SICA 略逊于 SC。模型在 ARC 上 baseline 极高(96.67%)，接近天花板——高准确率区间多样性低，SICA 无操作空间。与 StrategyQA (+3.33pp n.s.) 对比：简单任务（高 baseline）SICA 无效或有害，中等难度任务有微弱正向趋势但不显著。整体支持 capability trap：模型能力越强/任务越简单 → SC 已近最优 → SICA 无增量。\\\"}, \\\"exp-009-overhead-analysis\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T12:01:08.521Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"SICA overhead 分析 (804题)：总时间 61.6s/题，其中 trace 生成 31.6s (51.3%)，约束提取 29.0s (47.0%)，去重 1.0s (1.6%)，MaxSAT 0.003s (≈0%)。SICA vs SC 多花 30.0s/题 (+48.7%)，开销几乎全部来自 constraint extraction LLM 调用。MaxSAT 求解可忽略。论文 insight：SICA 的计算瓶颈不在 formal reasoning (SAT) 而在 LLM extraction，且 extraction 产出的约束缺乏判别力——高成本低回报。\\\"}, \\\"exp-020-llama8b-folio-204\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T11:50:57.467Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"DEFINITIVE NEGATIVE。LLaMA-8B FOLIO 204题：SICA 60.8% vs SC 63.2%, delta=-2.45pp, McNemar p=0.2668, CI [-5.88, +0.98]。Discordant 4:9 SICA 输多赢少。exp-011 FOLIO +10pp 在 n=204 完全反转为 -2.45pp——确认为 Type I error (n=30 随机波动)。C0=61.8% 高（SICA 有操作空间），但约束改票时反而引入更多错误(9 SC wins vs 4 SICA wins)。Sweet spot hypothesis 被否定。SICA≤SC everywhere 确认。\\\"}, \\\"pilot-contrastive-prompt\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-17T08:02:40.938Z\\\", SC baseline 最高，capability trap 确认。跨 seed SC vs delta Pearson r=-0.8973。\"}, \"exp-050b-mistral-icl-oracle\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T23:19:54.810Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"ICL Oracle (5 gold FOL examples) on Mistral-7B FOLIO-199: SICA=54.27% vs SC=57.79%, delta=-3.52pp (p=0.167, ns). ICL boosted SC from vanilla 54.41% (exp-033) to 57.79% (+3.38pp), but SICA did not benefit. ICL improves individual trace quality enough to boost majority vote, but the resulting constraints become less diverse/discriminative for MaxSAT weighting. Confirms capability trap: better extraction quality does not translate to SICA gains when it also reduces beneficial disagreement.\"}, \"exp-050-mistral-icl-oracle-folio199\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T23:19:53.802Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp-049b-mistral-logiqa200\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T23:16:15.080Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Mistral-7B LogiQA-200: SICA=51.50% vs SC=51.00%, delta=+0.50pp (p=1.000, ns). Mistral's +4.90pp FOLIO advantage (exp-033) does not transfer to LogiQA. SC baseline 51% is near random for 4-choice MCQ. Consistent with exp-038 (14B LogiQA delta=-0.50pp): SICA provides no benefit on LogiQA regardless of model, likely because LogiQA's pragmatic reasoning resists symbolic constraint formalization.\"}, \"exp-045b-backward-verify\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:30:28.631Z\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\"}, \"exp-045-backward-verification\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:30:00.573Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"v1: 可能使用了 Qwen-7B 残留 vLLM (exp-035 遗留 port 8003)。BV=53.92% vs SC=75.00%, delta=-21.08pp。Z3 SAT rate 95.3%=无判别力。True bias 72.5% vs gold 35.3%。根因：backward FOL generation 质量同样差，验证方向不解决 capability trap。即使换正确模型 (-21pp 幅度过大) 可能仍 negative。建议 Director 决策是否用 Qwen2.5-14B 重跑 v2。\"}, \"exp-038-14b-logiqa200-16639\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:27:39.296Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"实验成功完成200/200题，之前被自动监控误判为failed。结果：SICA略逊于SC(-0.5%)，McNemar p=1.0完全不显著。仅3例disagreement(sica_only=1, sc_only=2)。extraction_rate=86.4%低于FOLIO实验(~90%+)，fail_empty=12.2%较高——LogiQA的非形式化推理对constraint extraction更难。contradiction_rate仅0.8%，几乎无矛盾检测。结论：SICA在LogiQA非形式逻辑推理上无增益，capability trap假设在此数据集上未验证。\"}, \"exp-033\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:25:06.703Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Mistral-7B FOLIO-204: SICA 59.31% vs SC 54.41%, delta=+4.90pp (McNemar p=0.013, significant). 首次 SICA 显著超越 SC。关键因素：C0=81.37%（远高于 Qwen-7B 的 55%），高 diversity 使 SC majority vote 退化，而 SICA MaxSAT 仍能从 noisy traces 中提取信号。Discordant: SICA-only=12, SC-only=2, 高度不对称。但绝对准确率低（59% vs Qwen-7B 77%），说明 Mistral-7B 基础推理能力弱。结论：capability trap 非普遍现象，依赖 diversity/quality 平衡——高 diversity + 低 quality 时 SICA 有优势。\"}, \"exp-042b-premise-formal\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T20:10:14.058Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"失败根因：run_premise_formalization.sh 参数顺序是 PORT GPU，但调用传了 GPU PORT (2 8002)，导致 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=8002 → NVMLError_InvalidArgument。vLLM 未启动，0 题完成。将在 cleanup Worker 中用显式命令重部署。\"}, \"exp-044-icl-oracle\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:54:58.560Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"v2 失败: vLLM server 连接中断 (httpx.ConnectError: All connection attempts failed)，EXIT_CODE=129 (SIGHUP)。实验跑完但数据无效：extraction 成功率仅 17.1% (307/1800, 1490 json_fail)，SC=46.23% 远低于 Qwen2.5-14B 预期 75.49%。结果不可用，需在 vLLM 稳定后重跑。worker_infra_westd25068-gpu-cleanup-redeploy 正在处理 westd-25068 基础设施。\"}, \"exp-035\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:07:57.772Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"v2 完成: Qwen2.5-7B FOLIO-204, 203/204题有结果(1题缺失)。JSON summary是stale的(n=195时计算)，实际数据从results数组203条计算。SICA与SC仅3个discordant pairs，几乎完全一致。假设验证：SICA无法outperform SC on 7B，delta=-0.49pp (p=1.0)。C0=0.55高于14B但未能转化为SICA优势。注意7B SC(77.34%)意外高于14B SC(75.49%)，可能因FOLIO数据特性。\"}, \"exp-036b-cross-trace-unsat\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:04:55.982Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Cross-trace UNSAT 验证了 Director 假设：cross-answer UNSAT rate (8.7%) 约为 within-trace (0.9%) 的 10x。但主要瓶颈是 Z3 变量命名不一致：56.4% 的 answer pairs 共享零变量，Z3 无法跨 trace 比较。False-True 对最具辨别力 (non-empty 中 46.7% UNSAT)。最终对准确率无影响（仅覆盖 9/204 问题），第 6 个 negative constructive。核心诊断价值：变量命名不一致是 capability trap 的关键机制之一。\"}, \"exp-042-premise-formalization\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T19:02:37.081Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp-043-consistency-filtering\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:59:10.069Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Consistency filtering NEGATIVE: 过滤后约束数量骤降（T≥4 仅 1.8/题, T≥8 仅 0.3/题），MaxSAT 无法区分答案，退化为 SC。根因：Z3 变量命名不一致导致语义等价约束被视为不同（avg 13.2 unique constraints/problem），跨 trace 一致性极低。第 5 个 negative constructive intervention。注意：T=1 的 77.94% 与 exp-001 的 74.51% 差异是已知 pipeline 非确定性问题（code path confound）。\"}, \"exp-036-z3-feedback-folio204\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:53:05.656Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z3 feedback mechanism had zero real impact. Apparent +3.0pp (77.5% vs 74.5%) entirely due to code path confound: JSON serialization vs in-memory processing caused different dedup/scoring behavior. UNSAT trigger rate 0.9% (22/2448 traces), too low for feedback loop to meaningfully activate. All observed delta on discordant problems came from pipeline non-determinism, not Z3 refinement. Fourth negative constructive intervention after contrastive, cross-model, generational.\"}, \"exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:06:48.306Z\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"notes\": \"Killed per D049: extraction_rate 73% below 80% threshold. Partial results (35/204): SICA 88.57%, SC 85.71%, delta +2.86pp. But extraction_rate degrading and ETA 16h+ made continued run not worthwhile. GPU freed for D050 constructive experiments.\"}, \"exp-033-qwen3-pw600-nonthinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:00:09.156Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: AutoDL 平台级 SIGTERM 导致 vLLM EngineCore 崩溃 (00:05:54)，后紧急清理 Worker 误杀 tmux server 连带终止。518/600 intermediates 有效可恢复。\"}, \"exp-004-qwen7b-rerun\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:00:08.383Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: ModelScope 下载失败，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 模型下载未完成。实验从未开始运行。需要 HF 镜像修复后重试。\"}, \"exp-007-qwen72b-awq\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:00:07.574Z\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"notes\": \"FAILED: HuggingFace 网络不通 (Network unreachable)，模型下载失败。72B AWQ 模型从未下载完成，实验从未开始运行。需要 HF 镜像修复后重试。\"}, \"exp-027-qwen3-14b-non-thinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T17:58:11.787Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Qwen3-14B non-thinking FOLIO-204: SICA 84.31% vs SC 83.82%, delta=+0.49pp。Qwen3 SC baseline 83.82% 远高于 Qwen2.5-14B 的 75.00% (+8.82pp)，证明 Qwen3 基础能力显著提升。但 SICA-SC gap 仍≈0 (+0.49pp)，capability trap 在次世代模型上持续。Extraction rate 99.5% (vs 14B 98.5%)，contradiction rate 2.83%。论文价值：(1) 证明 capability trap 是结构性的，非模型能力不足；(2) 代际升级提升 SC 不提升 SICA delta。\"}, \"exp-030-crossmodel-mistral-folio204\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T16:16:11.345Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"NEGATIVE. Cross-model extraction (Mistral-7B extracts constraints from Qwen2.5-14B traces) 完全无效：SICA = SC = 75.00%, discriminative_rate=0%, recovery_rate=0%. Mistral 虽然是不同模型家族，但提取的 constraints 同样缺乏判别力。这进一步强化 capability trap 结论：问题不在 same-model bias，而在 LLM extraction 本身的能力限制——无论哪个模型做 extraction，都无法从 CoT traces 中提取出对答案有判别力的 symbolic constraints。论文 claim: cross-model 不是 rescue path。\"}, \"exp-031-oracle-folio-regen\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T15:55:28.070Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"KEY RESULT. Oracle-SICA (gold FOL + Z3 entailment + consistency-weighted voting) 89.71% vs SC 75.49% vs SICA(self) 74.51%, +14.22pp over SC, McNemar p=2.0e-07 极显著, b=29 c=0 (oracle 从未输给 SC)。Z3 gold agreement 95.10% (194/204), FOL parser 204/204 全成功。Per-type: True 97.2% (+4.1pp), False 93.6% (+6.3pp), Unknown 78.3% (+31.9pp)。与 exp-021 (88.24%, 旧 traces) 对比：oracle 准确率从 88.24% 提升至 89.71%（+1.47pp），delta 从 +8.82pp 增至 +14.22pp。差异来源：(1) exp-026 traces 的 SC baseline 更低 (75.49% vs 79.41%)；(2) Z3 agreement 从 94.6% 提升至 95.1%。结论与 exp-021 一致：MAX-SAT 聚合机制有效——瓶颈在约束提取质量。\"}, \"exp-029-contrastive-folio204\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T15:38:58.925Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\"}, \"exp-026-folio204-trace-regen\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T14:46:34.862Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"FOLIO 204 全量 trace 重生成完成 (204/204)。SICA 74.51% vs SC 75.00%, delta=-0.49pp，与 sica_expanded_14b FOLIO 子集 (79.4%/79.4%, delta=0) 一致——capability trap 在重生成 traces 上仍成立。归一化 bug 修复 + 温度双参数分离后 extraction_rate=98.53% (vs 之前 89%)。Traces 已被 exp-029 contrastive 实验复用。结果路径: /root/symb_invariant_consensus/results/folio_204_14b/folio_204_results.json\"}, \"exp-023-discriminative-rate\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T04:44:24.397Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Full 804-instance discriminative rate (R3 W1+W3, v1+v2 合并). ```text
Key findings:

1. **westd-16639 GPU mapping**:
   - PID 413036 (VLLM::EngineCore, 84216 MiB) → GPU 0: Mistral-7B (port 8000)
   - PID 413844 (VLLM::EngineCore, 83556 MiB) → GPU 1: Qwen3-14B (port 8001)
   - PID 435354 (VLLM::EngineCore, 88106 MiB) → GPU 2: Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ (port 8002)
   - PID 449434 (VLLM::EngineCore, 88228 MiB) → GPU 3: R1-Distill-8B vLLM (port 8003)

GPU 3 is consuming 88GB of memory but showing zero utilization, which means the R1-Distill vLLM server is loaded but idle.

The R1-Distill model was spun up for the pipeline rerun that's already finished, and since the cross-extraction actually uses Mistral on port 8000, this instance is just sitting there wasting GPU resources. Though something's off — an 8B model shouldn't need 88GB of VRAM, so I'm wondering if this is actually R1-Distill or if there's a mislabeling issue here. 8B in BF16 should be ~16GB. Maybe this is using a large model allocation or something else is going on.

Wait, looking more carefully at the summary context: "kill R1-Distill vLLM (cuda:3) → deploy Mistral port 8004 → run reextract_bpe_fix.py for remaining ~111 problems". The plan was to kill R1-Distill vLLM after the pipeline finished, then deploy a second Mistral instance. But the pipeline has finished ("FULL_MVP_DONE") and the R1-Distill vLLM is STILL running (PID 449110, 2h31m), occupying GPU 3 at 88GB with 0% utilization.

Actually wait, 88GB for an 8B model makes no sense. Let me check what model is actually loaded on port 8003. It might be that port 8002 was actually being used by R1-Distill instead... No, the env probe said Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ was on port 8002. But R1-Distill's metrics.json had wrong metadata claiming it was "Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ" — that's a metadata contamination issue I identified earlier.

Actually, the 88GB allocation makes sense now — vLLM pre-allocates GPU memory for KV cache, so even an 8B model with gpu_memory_utilization=0.9 will reserve most of the GPU's capacity. 88GB out of 98GB is exactly ~90%, which is the default vLLM behavior on this card.

So the real problem is that R1-Distill's vLLM is running but completely idle (0% utilization). The pipeline rerun finished, and the cross-extraction is using Mistral on port 8000 instead of R1-Distill. GPU 3 is being wasted.

Looking at the cross-extraction progress in per_trace_constraints_v2, I'm seeing files from folio_0 through folio_137, plus folio_195-203, but there are gaps in between — no folio_138 through folio_194. That's roughly 138 files plus 9 more, but the missing range suggests the total is around 147 files processed so far.

Let me recount the files more carefully... Ah, I see the issue now—the files are listed alphabetically, not numerically, so folio_10 comes before folio_2. The highest file is folio_203, with gaps between folio_84 to folio_99 and possibly others in the higher ranges.

Looking at the actual count, there are about 131 files present out of 204 total, meaning 73 are missing. The v2 extraction added roughly 38 new files since the original 93, which suggests the process has slowed significantly or stalled. I'm checking the running processes to see if extraction is still active—there's a Python process from 13 minutes ago that might be a new worker, and another from 8+ hours ago.

The 70B pipeline on westd-16639 has progressed to 18 intermediates, which aligns with its 4h48m runtime at about 17 minutes per problem. However, at that pace, completing all 204 problems would take roughly 58 hours, which won't meet the deadline. Meanwhile, the model download on westd-25068 shows partial shard files in the temp directory—the old 37 shards are gone and being replaced with individual model weight files, though the download appears incomplete. just started. Given the slow download speed of 1.7-3MB/s from the previous worker, this 100GB model will take roughly 9-16 hours to fully download. I need to check whether the R1-Distill cross-extraction is still running, if there's a new extraction process, and what's happening with the API experiments—plus investigate that 13-minute-old Python process on PID 463541 as a potential new worker, and determine if GPU 3 is actually idle or if the pipeline has other work queued.

If the R1-Distill inference is done and no more work is coming, GPU 3 is sitting unused, which is a resource waste I should document.

_source line 2798 · 2026-05-21T17:28:06.290Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Key findings:

1. **R1-Distill cross-extraction progress**: The `find -newer` shows files up to folio_140. The `ls` listing missed folio_138-140 because of alphabetical sorting but the `find` command shows they exist. So the extraction is continuing and has reached at least folio_140. Still 64 files to go (folio_141-194). The `reextraction_summary.json` still says 93 problems — it's a stale summary from the original extraction, not updated for the v2 run.

   Wait, the `ls` listing showed files folio_0 through folio_137, plus folio_195-203. 

   Looking more carefully at the `ls` output, I see folio_83, folio_9, folio_100-137, and folio_195-203, but folio_138 and beyond are missing from that listing. The `find -newer` command is showing a different picture though — it's finding files up to folio_140, which means they do exist even though they didn't show up in my `ls` output.

   This tells me the extraction process is actively running right now and creating new files as I check. The current count is around 134 files, with significant gaps in the folio_84-99 range and folio_141-194 range still unaccounted for. The extraction appears to be working sequentially from folio_100 upward, filling in the middle section while the original batch of files (folio_0-83 and scattered others) was already there.

   Looking at the other pipelines, the 70B model has only processed 18 intermediates so far and at its current rate of ~17 minutes per problem, it won't finish in time for the deadline—that's roughly 58 hours total. The gpt-5.5 and o3 processes don't have run logs yet, so I need to check their status.

   The westd-25068 72B model download is still in progress with only 7 temp shards downloaded (~4.87GB), and one shard appears corrupted or incomplete. Meanwhile, GPU 3 is being wasted—the R1-Distill vLLM is sitting idle at 88GB while the cross-extraction actually uses Mistral on GPU 0, so I should kill that process to free up the GPU.

   Looking at the registry notes for exp-d126-r1-distill-sica, the plan was to kill R1-Distill on GPU 3 and deploy Mistral on port 8004 after the pipeline finishes. But the reextraction is already running Mistral on port 8000, so either the plan got updated or the second deployment isn't needed—either way, GPU 3 is sitting idle right now.

   The real concern is whether the cross-extraction and the model-sica experiment are both hitting the same Mistral instance on port 8000, which would create contention between them. I need to verify if PID 463541 is actually the extraction process, then check on the gpt-5.5 and o3 progress.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Good findings:

1. **PID 463541** = `python reextract_bpe_fix.py --intermediates-dir results/exp_r1_distill_8b_sica/intermediates --output-dir results/exp_r1_distill_8b_sica/per_trace_constraints_v2 --api-base http://localhost:8000/v1`
   
   So the reextraction process IS using Mistral on port 8000 (GPU 0). It's only 13 minutes old. This is a new worker that just started the reextraction. The original plan was to deploy a second Mistral on port 8004 (GPU 3), but instead they're using the existing Mistral on port 8000.
   
   I'm seeing a resource contention problem here: GPU 0's Mistral is now handling both the long-running crossmodel-sica job and the new reextraction task, while GPU 3 sits completely idle with the R1-Distill vLLM consuming 88GB of memory. I should kill that idle vLLM and spin up a second Mistral instance on GPU 3 to handle the reextraction work separately.

There's also a 70B pipeline running on GPU 2 (Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ via port 8002) executing the full MVP with FOLIO data, K=12, temperature 0.7, and seed 42 — this one's isolated and not competing for resources.

Looking at the progress on the 70B run, it's only completed 18 out of 204 problems in nearly 5 hours, which puts us at roughly 16 minutes per problem. That means the remaining 186 problems would take another 50 hours or so — definitely not finishing before May 25. I should check the checkpoint files for the GPT-5.5 and o3 experiments to see where they stand, and also verify that the R1-Distill extraction is still moving forward.
```

_source line 2857 · 2026-05-21T17:29:51.730Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the full picture:

**gpt-5.5**: 204/204 complete, 185/204 problems have all 12 valid answers (19 problems have <12 valid). SC = 172/204 = 84.31%.

**o3**: 204/204 complete, only 109/204 problems have all 12 valid answers (95 problems have <12 valid). This is a severe empty trace problem — 46.6% of problems have fewer than 12 valid traces.

Let me count the o3 n_valid_answers distribution:


Analyzing the distribution, I see a concerning pattern: 7 problems have zero valid traces at all, with another 4 having just one valid trace. The rest are scattered across 2-9 valid answers, with clusters around 6-7 and 9 valid answers, but nothing approaching the 12 we need.

The real issue here is that o3 has a severe empty trace problem — when there are no valid answers to work with, the system likely falls back to some default behavior, which artificially deflates the 79.41% SC accuracy metric.

For gpt-5.5, the situation is better but still worth noting: 185 out of 204 problems have all 12 valid traces, meaning 19 problems are incomplete. I should check whether any of these have zero valid answers like o3 does.

The key takeaway is that o3's 79.41% accuracy is likely artificially suppressed because problems with no valid traces get counted as failures, whereas gpt-5.5's 84.31% is more reliable since it has far fewer of these problematic cases.

Beyond the model results, there are several operational issues I need to flag: an idle GPU consuming massive resources that should be terminated, the R1-Distill extraction still in progress, and two separate model runs with very different performance profiles — one moving at a reasonable pace and another that's far too slow to meet the deadline. I should compile these findings into a concise report for the Director.
```

I'm identifying instances where the value is 0 in this sequence—these represent problems with no valid answers. The real issue is that self-consistency voting can't function when there are zero valid traces to vote on. I need to check whether these 8 zero-trace problems are actually a subset of the 42 incorrect entries to understand the actual impact on the overall score.

The o3 model has a more concerning profile—while it has complete data coverage, it only achieves 79.41% accuracy and suffers from a significant data quality problem where 8 problems have no valid traces at all, making their self-consistency scores undefined.

The root cause is that o3 returns empty responses for roughly a third of API calls, which artificially deflates its reported accuracy. This needs to be cleaned before the data can be reliably used. Key findings from independent verification:

1. **crossmodel_metrics.json (204 problems):**
   - model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B
   - extraction: cross-model (Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3)
   - n_problems: 204

   - **sica_accuracy: 0.7696** (157/204)
   - **delta_pp: 2.45** — worker reported +1.96pp but the actual file says +2.45pp!
   - extraction_rate: 0.9334, with 47 SICA errors and a mean branching ratio of 2.92 across 26 samples
   - inter-rater agreement shows process_kappa_tfidf at 0.2477 but answer_fleiss_kappa at 0.6944

2. **br_kappa_metrics.json (self-extraction, 204 problems):**
   - Both SC and SICA collapsed to 0.3235, indicating the BPE tokenization broke the extraction entirely with only 1.28% recovery rate

3. **per_trace_constraints_v2**: All 204 files are accounted for in the dataset.

**CRITICAL DISCREPANCY:**

The worker reported SICA as 0.7647 with a 1.96 percentage point gap, but the actual crossmodel_metrics.json shows SICA at 0.7696 with a 2.45 percentage point gap—they got SC correct at 0.7451 but understated both the SICA value and the delta between metrics.

Let me verify this by working backward from the file data: SC of 0.7451 equals 152 out of 204 cases, while SICA of 0.7696 equals 157 out of 204 cases, giving a delta of 5 cases or 2.45 percentage points. The worker's reported SICA of 0.7647 would correspond to exactly 156 out of 204, meaning they're off by one case on the SICA count.

Now looking at the 93-problem subset, there's a much larger inconsistency: the worker reports SC as 0.7527 and SICA as 0.7849, but the original file shows SC as 0.7312 and SICA as 0.7527. That's a 2.15 percentage point difference for SC alone. What's striking is that the worker's SC value (0.7527) exactly matches the original file's SICA value—this suggests the worker might have accidentally swapped or misaligned which metrics correspond to which dataset.

The root cause is likely that the original crossmodel_summary.json used a different SC baseline from the R1-Distill pipeline rerun (73.12%), while the worker may have recomputed SC using only problems with successful cross-extraction, creating a filtered subset. However, the authoritative source is crossmodel_metrics.json, which reports Δ = +2.45pp—actually larger than the worker's +1.96pp, which is good news for the effect size.

Interestingly, when expanding from 93 to 204 problems, the delta increased from +2.15pp to +2.45pp, contradicting the worker's report of a decrease from +3.22pp to +1.96pp. This discrepancy suggests the worker used different baseline numbers for the 93-problem set. The authoritative comparison shows SC improving from 73.12% to 74.51% and SICA from 75.27% to 76.96% across the full dataset. The kappa coefficient stayed consistently low around 0.25, confirming high diversity in the extraction process, and the mean branching ratio aligns with expectations. The self-extraction metrics show it's completely broken with only 1.28% extraction rate. So the critical finding is that R1-Distill is the only model where cross-model extraction actually improves SICA over SC by 2.45 percentage points on the full 204-problem set, which validates that low process diversity is essential for this escape mechanism to work. I need to flag the discrepancy in the worker's reported delta when I present these results.

Breaking down the new 111 problems added to the dataset, SC accuracy comes to 84/111 (75.68%) and SICA accuracy to 87/111 (78.38%), giving a delta of 3/111 or +2.70pp. Interestingly, this larger delta on the newer problems suggests the effect is holding up well and possibly even strengthening.

Now I'm noticing a discrepancy in the worker's reported numbers for the original 93 problems—they reported SC=0.7527 and SICA=0.7849, which translates to 70/93 and 73/93 respectively. Regardless, the 204-problem dataset is the authoritative source: SC comes to 74.51% and SICA to 76.96%, a difference of 2.45 percentage points. I need to note that the worker's report contains these inconsistencies.

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — exp-d126-r1-distill-sica (worker_experiment_r1-distill-extract-final)\n\n**结论**: WARNING（数据有效，但 Worker 报告数字有误差）\n\n### 独立核实\n\n从 `crossmodel_metrics.json`（204 条逐题记录）逐条 grep 计数：\n\n| 指标 | Reviewer 核实值 | Worker 报告值 | 差异 |\n|------|----------------|--------------|------|\n| SC correct | 152/204 = **74.51%** | 74.51% | ✓ |\n| SICA correct | **157/204 = 76.96%** | 156/204 = 76.47% | **差 1 题** |\n| Δ(SICA−SC) | **+2.45pp** | +1.96pp | **差 0.49pp** |\n| extraction_rate | 93.34% | 93.3% | ✓ |\n| Process-κ | 0.2477 | 0.2477 | ✓ |\n| Mean BR | 2.9183 | 2.85 | Worker 来源不明 |\n| n_sica_wrong | 47 | — | — |\n| n_br_samples | 26 | — | — |\n\n- [severity: warning] **Worker 报告的 SICA=76.47%（Δ=+1.96pp）与实际数据文件 SICA=76.96%（Δ=+2.45pp）不一致。** crossmodel_metrics.json 明确记录 sica_accuracy=0.7696、delta_pp=2.45。grep 逐条计数 sica_correct=true 得 157 条、false 得 47 条，合计 204。Worker 比实际少算了 1 题。**论文和 ROADMAP 应使用文件中的 Δ=+2.45pp。**\n\n- [severity: warning] **Worker 93题对比基线与 crossmodel_summary.json 不一致。** 原始 crossmodel_summary.json: SC=73.12%, SICA=75.27%, Δ=+2.15pp。Worker 报告 93题: SC=75.27%, SICA=78.49%, Δ=+3.22pp。Worker 的 93题 \"SC\" 恰等于原始的 \"SICA\"（70/93），疑似标签互换或使用了不同计算方法。**建议以 crossmodel_metrics.json 的 204 题数据为准，弃用 Worker 自行计算的 93 题数据。**\n\n### 核实结论\n\n- [severity: info] **R1-Distill cross-model SICA > SC +2.45pp 在全 FOLIO-204 上成立。** 这是唯一 Δ>0 的 cross-model 配置（对比 gpt-4.1→Mistral: Δ=-1.47pp, Qwen14B→Mistral: Δ=0pp）。关键区分因子：Process-κ=0.25（远低于 gpt-4.1 的 0.79），验证了\"trace diversity 是 cross-model escape 的必要条件\"的核心叙事。\n\n- [severity: info] **新增 111 题的 Δ 比原始 93 题更大**（+2.70pp vs +2.15pp），效应在更大样本上稳健乃至增强。\n\n- [severity: info] **完整指标汇总**：\n  - SC=152/204(74.51%), SICA=157/204(76.96%), Δ=+2.45pp\n  - Mean BR=2.92, Median BR=2.11（n_br_finite=26）\n  - Process-κ=0.248, Answer Fleiss'κ=0.694\n  - Extraction rate=93.3%（14602 total / 8653 unique constraints）",

3. Frontier SC ranking table is informative.
4. GPU status updated: cuda:3 being repurposed for new Mistral deployment.

**Issues to check:**

Now I'm cross-checking the R1-Distill metrics against the crossmodel_metrics.json data I reviewed earlier—SC=74.51%, SICA=76.96%, Δ=+2.45pp all validate correctly, with BR=2.9183 as reported.

92× and both kappa values (process=0.248, answer=0.694) matching the source data.

Looking at the running section, the Mistral extraction on cuda:3 confirms the R1-Distill vLLM was shut down to free up that GPU, which addresses my earlier concern about resource waste. The 70B AWQ download seems optimistic given only ~5GB is staged in temp so far, while SICA original is progressing at 18/204 on cuda:2 and gemini is around 20/204. The o3 data cleaning is still pending.

The cross-model comparison correctly captures the divergent kappa outcomes: R1→Mistral shows improvement (κ=0.248, +2.45pp) while gpt-4.1→Mistral shows degradation (κ=0.792, -1.47pp). The o3 SC=79.41% is appropriately flagged with an asterisk noting the 33% empty traces caveat.

The 70B AWQ ETA of ~1h looks suspicious—based on the previous download speed of 1.7-3MB/s and ~100GB total, this should take 9-16 hours, not 1 hour, unless there's a different download method or accelerated transfer happening that I'm not accounting for.

Now I need to verify whether the R1-Distill vLLM on GPU 3 was actually terminated to make room for gpt-5.5 extraction, check the current extraction status, and look into the statistical significance of the R1-Distill improvement—specifically whether the McNemar p-value for the 152 vs 157 correct predictions out of 204 samples is actually significant given the discordant pair distribution.

The R1-Distill vLLM has been successfully terminated and replaced with the new deployment on GPU 3. The Qwen3-14B model remains loaded on GPU 1 despite showing zero utilization, suggesting it's being held for the crossmodel-sica process.

GPU 1 sitting idle while GPU 0 is maxed out is a red flag. The bidirectional extraction experiment between Mistral and Qwen3 should be using both GPUs, so either the Qwen3→Mistral phase completed and we've moved into the Mistral→Qwen3 phase that only needs GPU 0, the whole experiment finished, or something stalled. I need to verify whether PID 414515 is still actively running.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The crossmodel-sica experiment is still actively running. The log shows at 01:56:47 UTC:
- "Generated 12 Mistral traces" — generating Mistral traces
- "Mistral same-model extraction: 58 constraints" — self-extraction
- "Mistral->Qwen3 cross-extraction: 215 constraints" — cross-extraction Mistral→Qwen3

Importantly, all requests are going to port 8000 (Mistral), not port 8001 (Qwen3). 

So GPU 1 (Qwen3-14B) is idle because Phase 2 is extracting constraints from the Qwen3 traces that were generated in Phase 1, but doing that extraction with Mistral rather than Qwen3 itself. The stored traces are just being read and processed by Mistral running on GPU 0. The arrow notation is ambiguous—"Mistral→Qwen3" could mean either Qwen3 extracting from Mistral traces or Mistral extracting from Qwen3 traces. Since all HTTP requests are hitting port 8000 (Mistral), the current phase is likely Mistral extracting from Qwen3 traces, which would correspond to the `extraction_qwen3_cross_mistral` directory.

This explains why GPU 1 (Qwen3) appears idle—Phase 2 only uses Mistral, so it's not a problem, just expected behavior for this extraction phase.

Now I'm reviewing the ROADMAP against the actual observations. The document is largely accurate: the R1-Distill metrics are correct, the gpt-5.5 and o3 SC values check out, and GPU 3 has been repurposed with a new Mistral deployment running at full capacity (confirmed by the process ID).

There are a few cautions though. The 70B AWQ download estimate of around an hour is probably too optimistic given we're only at 20GB of 100GB so far, though the download rate is picking up. GPU 1 sitting idle makes sense during this Mistral-only phase. The o3 empty trace issue is acknowledged but there's no concrete cleanup plan yet.

Looking at the timeline for the next few days—Paper Agent V16 Patch on May 22, compile on May 23, review on May 24, submit on May 25—it's tight but feasible. The critical blocker is whether gpt-5.5 extraction finishes in time, since everything else depends on that completing first.
```
